Mtouaa Studio — independent Android creative suite
Publisher: Mtouaa Labs. Website: https://mtouaa.com

This edition is based on the open-source PhotoCraft, VectorCraft, FilmCraft, LightCraft,
PdfCraft, EffectCraft, DesignCraft, FigCraft, TextureCraft, WordCraft, SoundCraft, DeckCraft,
CADCraft, GridCraft and Craft Anything projects by their respective contributors.
The source repositories and exact source commits are recorded in each editor's
mtouaa-build.json file. Source patches and build instructions accompany this Android project.

Modified 3 November 2026: upstream protected ArtCraft branding and shortcut artwork were
replaced with original Mtouaa artwork; editor display names and publisher links were changed.
The underlying open-source projects, contributors, copyright statements and required
licences remain credited. This is not an official release of those projects and is not
affiliated with or endorsed by the ArtCraft Team or Mozilla.

The editor code retains its upstream MIT OR Apache-2.0 and dependency/asset/font licences.
Per-editor LICENSE, NOTICE, ATTRIBUTION and OFL files remain bundled. Modification notices
identify this independently built edition. Original artwork is described in
APP_ICON_ATTRIBUTION.md. The original trademark assets are not included in this edition.

Mozilla GeckoView 159.0.20261022150525 is included unmodified under MPL 2.0 and its
applicable third-party notices. Mozilla and Firefox are trademarks of the Mozilla Foundation.
MPL 2.0: https://www.mozilla.org/MPL/2.0/
Corresponding GeckoView source:
https://hg.mozilla.org/releases/mozilla-release/rev/5add844c26613d7e4d84c799d6e23cf987b1240d

objc2-core-text-0.3.2
https://crates.io/crates/objc2-core-text/0.3.2
SHA-256: 0cde0dfb48d25d2b4862161a4d5fcc0e3c24367869ad306b0c9ec0073bfed92d


objc2-core-text-0.3.2/LICENSE.md
# License

The licensing of these crates is a bit complicated:
- The crates `objc2`, `block2`, `objc2-foundation` and `objc2-encode` are
  [currently][#23] licensed under [the MIT license][MIT].
- All other crates are trio-licensed under the [Zlib], [Apache-2.0] or [MIT]
  license, at your option.

Furthermore, the crates are (usually automatically) derived from Apple SDKs,
and that may have implications for licensing, see below for details.

[#23]: https://github.com/madsmtm/objc2/issues/23
[MIT]: https://opensource.org/license/MIT
[Zlib]: https://zlib.net/zlib_license.html
[Apache-2.0]: https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0


## Apple SDKs

These crates are derived from Apple SDKs shipped with Xcode. You can obtain a
copy of the Xcode license at:

https://www.apple.com/legal/sla/docs/xcode.pdf

Or by typing `xcodebuild -license` in your terminal.

From reading the license, it is unclear whether distributing derived works
such as these crates are allowed?

But in any case, to practically use these crates, you will have to link, and
that only works when you have the correct Xcode SDK available to provide the
required `.tbd` files, which is why we choose to still use the normal SPDX
identifiers in the crates (Xcode is required to use the crates, and when using
Xcode you have already agreed to the Xcode license).

  the data are distributed unmodified and Fogra is identified as the source. The designation
  FOGRAxx may be used solely to identify the respective reference data. Such use does not
  imply certification, approval or endorsement by Fogra."
crates/text/assets/hyphen/en-us.dic: public domain, derived from the Moby Hyphenator II list by
  Grady Ward (Project Gutenberg eBook #3204).
crates/text/assets/hyphen/en-us.pat: CC0-1.0, trained from en-us.dic by DesignCraft's trainer.
Hyphenation patterns for other languages, embedded through the hypher crate only when the
  matching craft-text feature is enabled (none are by default). They come from hyph-utf8,
  https://github.com/hyphenation/tex-hyphen, as packaged in https://github.com/typst/hypher
  (patterns/). Each file is under the license stated in its header: LPPL, MIT, BSD-3-Clause, the
  Unlicense, a notice permitting any use, or a choice of licenses that includes one of these. An
  application that enables a language ships that file's copyright and license notice. The
  exception is Slovak (hyph-sk.tex, Copyright (C) 1992 Jana Chlebíková), under the GNU General
  Public License, version 2 or later (SPDX: GPL-2.0-or-later). craft-text asks for it through the
  feature "slovak-gpl", which "all-languages" does not include; an application that enables it
  ships GPL-licensed data. Cargo unifies features, so another dependency that enables hypher's
  "slovak" feature (hypher's default features do) embeds the same data; a build that must exclude
  it checks its dependency graph. The Spanish patterns (feature "spanish") are hyph-es.tex 5.0.
  Copyright (C) 1993, 1997 Javier Bezos, 2001-2019 Javier Bezos, CervanTeX. MIT License:
  Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy of this software
  and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal in the Software without
  restriction, including without limitation the rights to use, copy, modify, merge, publish,
  distribute, sublicense, and/or sell copies of the Software, and to permit persons to whom the
  Software is furnished to do so, subject to the following conditions: The above copyright notice
  and this permission notice shall be included in all copies or substantial portions of the
  Software. THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS", WITHOUT WARRANTY OF ANY KIND, EXPRESS OR IMPLIED,
  INCLUDING BUT NOT LIMITED TO THE WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY, FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE
  AND NONINFRINGEMENT. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE AUTHORS OR COPYRIGHT HOLDERS BE LIABLE FOR ANY CLAIM,
  DAMAGES OR OTHER LIABILITY, WHETHER IN AN ACTION OF CONTRACT, TORT OR OTHERWISE, ARISING FROM,
  OUT OF OR IN CONNECTION WITH THE SOFTWARE OR THE USE OR OTHER DEALINGS IN THE SOFTWARE.
- Word-break dictionaries and LSTM models for Thai, Lao, Khmer and Burmese, embedded through the
  icu_segmenter_data crate only when craft-text's "thai", "lao", "khmer" or "burmese" feature is
  enabled (none are by default). From ICU4X, https://github.com/unicode-org/icu4x.
  Copyright (c) 2020-2024 Unicode, Inc. Unicode License v3 (SPDX: Unicode-3.0); portions adapted
  from ICU4C, (c) 1995-2016 International Business Machines Corporation and others. An
  application that enables one of these features ships the Unicode License v3 copyright and
  permission notice, https://www.unicode.org/license.txt.

The optional PDF writer bundles four CC0 ICC profiles by Clinton Ingram. See ATTRIBUTION.md for per-file sources and hashes.

Optional PDF writer

Krilla 0.8.2 (https://github.com/LaurenzV/krilla), MIT OR Apache-2.0.
Upstream licence texts are retained in licenses/pdf-writer.
The optional writer embeds Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram
(https://github.com/saucecontrol/Compact-ICC-Profiles), under CC0-1.0.
See licenses/pdf-writer/LICENSE-ICC.txt and ATTRIBUTION.md for sources and hashes.
The writer adds no font files. Its four existing ICC profiles are retained under vendor/krilla/icc.

Krilla is vendored with the local patches in vendor/krilla/patches; see vendor/krilla/README.craft.md
and vendor/krilla/SOURCE.json. Its upstream MIT/Apache-2.0 licences and CC0 profiles are unchanged.

Optional PDF reader

Hayro 0.7.1, hayro-interpret 0.7.0, hayro-syntax 0.7.2, hayro-jbig2 0.3.0, hayro-cmap 0.1.0
and hayro-postscript 0.1.0 (https://github.com/LaurenzV/hayro), MIT OR Apache-2.0, retain their
upstream licence files. The first five are patched sources transferred from PdfCraft
(https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft): hayro-cmap from revision
a2ec27b4775f53c40cad3456936d9406611e3a8d, the others from revision
ec0a965074f376a5bf610435948e4e5184bf3299. hayro-postscript is the crates.io 0.1.0 package
(upstream revision a2354aebaf86f5ab13e5fd6b6b0521306488bf9d) with one local patch.
See vendor/README.craft-reader.md and each crate's SOURCE.json for provenance and local changes.
The hayro-interpret plate mode (separations, overprint and output-intent simulation) is ported
from PdfCraft pull request #2770 by Guillem Hermida, commit
6fcf2a4aca29e943e28cb69017ff8f87d00656c6 (https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft/pull/2770),
MIT OR Apache-2.0; crates/pdf/tests/plates.rs and tests/support/synthetic_icc.rs adapt its tests.
Knockout transparency groups in hayro-interpret and hayro are ported from PdfCraft pull request
#2974 by mtlcl410, commit bae69819594a6385a027fec1f67e6b3c36c259b4
(https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft/pull/2974), MIT OR Apache-2.0;
crates/pdf/tests/knockout.rs adapts its test.
The reader retains the existing Foxit/PDFium standard-14 font assets (BSD-3-Clause),
Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram (CC0-1.0), and dependency CMaps (BSD-3-Clause).
Their licences and per-file hashes are retained in vendor/hayro-interpret/assets,
vendor/hayro-cmap/assets, licenses/pdf-backend and ATTRIBUTION.md. These are existing reader dependency assets;
applications retain their own document-font selection and licensing policy.

The Canon CRW decoder in crates/crw (src/lib.rs, src/tables.rs, src/tests.rs, tests/) and its
derivation record (crates/crw/clean-room/) come from:
- LightCraft, crates/raw/src/vendor/crw.rs, crates/raw/src/vendor/crw_tables.rs,
  crates/raw/tests/crw_oracle.rs, crates/raw/tests/it/vendor_robust.rs and clean-room/crw/,
  https://github.com/storytold/lightcraft/pull/3021, revision 6ca439b5155820d2befeacd81ec0c6a80b2bd3bc
  (decoder ported at 70c755e42a65661401142819278d51b0c5977f7f; the record is identical at both).
  https://github.com/storytold/lightcraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the LightCraft contributors.
Its white-level plateau estimate (saturation_plateau) is LightCraft's vendor::white_from_data at the
same revision. The CRW compression was derived clean-room from CC0 sample files and the
maintainer's own photographs by black-box comparison with a reference decoder's command-line
output; no raw-decoder source was consulted (crates/crw/clean-room/sources.md).

  Source: Fogra (Fogra Forschungsinstitut für Medientechnologien e.V.), www.fogra.org, from
  https://fogra.org/fileadmin/files/7_downloads/Charakterisierungsdaten/FOGRA39_to_FOGRA60_v2.zip
  Fogra's terms: "Free use and redistribution of the Fogra characterisation data is permitted,

  FOGRAxx may be used solely to identify the respective reference data. Such use does not

  Grady Ward (Project Gutenberg eBook #3204).
crates/text/assets/hyphen/en-us.pat: CC0-1.0, trained from en-us.dic by DesignCraft's trainer.
Hyphenation patterns for other languages, embedded through the hypher crate only when the
  matching craft-text feature is enabled (none are by default). They come from hyph-utf8,
  https://github.com/hyphenation/tex-hyphen, as packaged in https://github.com/typst/hypher
  (patterns/). Each file is under the license stated in its header: LPPL, MIT, BSD-3-Clause, the
  Unlicense, a notice permitting any use, or a choice of licenses that includes one of these. An
  application that enables a language ships that file's copyright and license notice. The
  exception is Slovak (hyph-sk.tex, Copyright (C) 1992 Jana Chlebíková), under the GNU General
  Public License, version 2 or later (SPDX: GPL-2.0-or-later). craft-text asks for it through the
  feature "slovak-gpl", which "all-languages" does not include; an application that enables it
  ships GPL-licensed data. Cargo unifies features, so another dependency that enables hypher's
  "slovak" feature (hypher's default features do) embeds the same data; a build that must exclude
  it checks its dependency graph. The Spanish patterns (feature "spanish") are hyph-es.tex 5.0.
  Copyright (C) 1993, 1997 Javier Bezos, 2001-2019 Javier Bezos, CervanTeX. MIT License:
  Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy of this software
  and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal in the Software without
  restriction, including without limitation the rights to use, copy, modify, merge, publish,
  distribute, sublicense, and/or sell copies of the Software, and to permit persons to whom the
  Software is furnished to do so, subject to the following conditions: The above copyright notice
  and this permission notice shall be included in all copies or substantial portions of the
  Software. THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS", WITHOUT WARRANTY OF ANY KIND, EXPRESS OR IMPLIED,
  INCLUDING BUT NOT LIMITED TO THE WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY, FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE
  AND NONINFRINGEMENT. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE AUTHORS OR COPYRIGHT HOLDERS BE LIABLE FOR ANY CLAIM,
  DAMAGES OR OTHER LIABILITY, WHETHER IN AN ACTION OF CONTRACT, TORT OR OTHERWISE, ARISING FROM,
  OUT OF OR IN CONNECTION WITH THE SOFTWARE OR THE USE OR OTHER DEALINGS IN THE SOFTWARE.
- Word-break dictionaries and LSTM models for Thai, Lao, Khmer and Burmese, embedded through the
  icu_segmenter_data crate only when craft-text's "thai", "lao", "khmer" or "burmese" feature is
  enabled (none are by default). From ICU4X, https://github.com/unicode-org/icu4x.
  Copyright (c) 2020-2024 Unicode, Inc. Unicode License v3 (SPDX: Unicode-3.0); portions adapted
  from ICU4C, (c) 1995-2016 International Business Machines Corporation and others. An
  application that enables one of these features ships the Unicode License v3 copyright and

The optional PDF writer bundles four CC0 ICC profiles by Clinton Ingram. See ATTRIBUTION.md for per-file sources and hashes.

Optional PDF writer

Krilla 0.8.2 (https://github.com/LaurenzV/krilla), MIT OR Apache-2.0.
Upstream licence texts are retained in licenses/pdf-writer.
The optional writer embeds Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram
(https://github.com/saucecontrol/Compact-ICC-Profiles), under CC0-1.0.
See licenses/pdf-writer/LICENSE-ICC.txt and ATTRIBUTION.md for sources and hashes.
The writer adds no font files. Its four existing ICC profiles are retained under vendor/krilla/icc.

Krilla is vendored with the local patches in vendor/krilla/patches; see vendor/krilla/README.craft.md
and vendor/krilla/SOURCE.json. Its upstream MIT/Apache-2.0 licences and CC0 profiles are unchanged.

Optional PDF reader

Hayro 0.7.1, hayro-interpret 0.7.0, hayro-syntax 0.7.2, hayro-jbig2 0.3.0, hayro-cmap 0.1.0
and hayro-postscript 0.1.0 (https://github.com/LaurenzV/hayro), MIT OR Apache-2.0, retain their
upstream licence files. The first five are patched sources transferred from PdfCraft
(https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft): hayro-cmap from revision
a2ec27b4775f53c40cad3456936d9406611e3a8d, the others from revision
ec0a965074f376a5bf610435948e4e5184bf3299. hayro-postscript is the crates.io 0.1.0 package
(upstream revision a2354aebaf86f5ab13e5fd6b6b0521306488bf9d) with one local patch.
See vendor/README.craft-reader.md and each crate's SOURCE.json for provenance and local changes.
The hayro-interpret plate mode (separations, overprint and output-intent simulation) is ported
from PdfCraft pull request #2770 by Guillem Hermida, commit
6fcf2a4aca29e943e28cb69017ff8f87d00656c6 (https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft/pull/2770),
MIT OR Apache-2.0; crates/pdf/tests/plates.rs and tests/support/synthetic_icc.rs adapt its tests.
Knockout transparency groups in hayro-interpret and hayro are ported from PdfCraft pull request
#2974 by mtlcl410, commit bae69819594a6385a027fec1f67e6b3c36c259b4
(https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft/pull/2974), MIT OR Apache-2.0;
crates/pdf/tests/knockout.rs adapts its test.
The reader retains the existing Foxit/PDFium standard-14 font assets (BSD-3-Clause),
Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram (CC0-1.0), and dependency CMaps (BSD-3-Clause).
Their licences and per-file hashes are retained in vendor/hayro-interpret/assets,
vendor/hayro-cmap/assets, licenses/pdf-backend and ATTRIBUTION.md. These are existing reader dependency assets;
applications retain their own document-font selection and licensing policy.

The Canon CRW decoder in crates/crw (src/lib.rs, src/tables.rs, src/tests.rs, tests/) and its
derivation record (crates/crw/clean-room/) come from:
- LightCraft, crates/raw/src/vendor/crw.rs, crates/raw/src/vendor/crw_tables.rs,
  crates/raw/tests/crw_oracle.rs, crates/raw/tests/it/vendor_robust.rs and clean-room/crw/,
  https://github.com/storytold/lightcraft/pull/3021, revision 6ca439b5155820d2befeacd81ec0c6a80b2bd3bc
  (decoder ported at 70c755e42a65661401142819278d51b0c5977f7f; the record is identical at both).
  https://github.com/storytold/lightcraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the LightCraft contributors.
Its white-level plateau estimate (saturation_plateau) is LightCraft's vendor::white_from_data at the
same revision. The CRW compression was derived clean-room from CC0 sample files and the
maintainer's own photographs by black-box comparison with a reference decoder's command-line
output; no raw-decoder source was consulted (crates/crw/clean-room/sources.md).

  application that enables one of these features ships the Unicode License v3 copyright and
  permission notice, https://www.unicode.org/license.txt.

The optional PDF writer bundles four CC0 ICC profiles by Clinton Ingram. See ATTRIBUTION.md for per-file sources and hashes.

Optional PDF writer

Krilla 0.8.2 (https://github.com/LaurenzV/krilla), MIT OR Apache-2.0.
Upstream licence texts are retained in licenses/pdf-writer.
The optional writer embeds Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram
(https://github.com/saucecontrol/Compact-ICC-Profiles), under CC0-1.0.
See licenses/pdf-writer/LICENSE-ICC.txt and ATTRIBUTION.md for sources and hashes.
The writer adds no font files. Its four existing ICC profiles are retained under vendor/krilla/icc.

Krilla is vendored with the local patches in vendor/krilla/patches; see vendor/krilla/README.craft.md
and vendor/krilla/SOURCE.json. Its upstream MIT/Apache-2.0 licences and CC0 profiles are unchanged.

Optional PDF reader

Hayro 0.7.1, hayro-interpret 0.7.0, hayro-syntax 0.7.2, hayro-jbig2 0.3.0, hayro-cmap 0.1.0
and hayro-postscript 0.1.0 (https://github.com/LaurenzV/hayro), MIT OR Apache-2.0, retain their
upstream licence files. The first five are patched sources transferred from PdfCraft
(https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft): hayro-cmap from revision
a2ec27b4775f53c40cad3456936d9406611e3a8d, the others from revision
ec0a965074f376a5bf610435948e4e5184bf3299. hayro-postscript is the crates.io 0.1.0 package
(upstream revision a2354aebaf86f5ab13e5fd6b6b0521306488bf9d) with one local patch.
See vendor/README.craft-reader.md and each crate's SOURCE.json for provenance and local changes.
The hayro-interpret plate mode (separations, overprint and output-intent simulation) is ported
from PdfCraft pull request #2770 by Guillem Hermida, commit
6fcf2a4aca29e943e28cb69017ff8f87d00656c6 (https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft/pull/2770),
MIT OR Apache-2.0; crates/pdf/tests/plates.rs and tests/support/synthetic_icc.rs adapt its tests.
Knockout transparency groups in hayro-interpret and hayro are ported from PdfCraft pull request
#2974 by mtlcl410, commit bae69819594a6385a027fec1f67e6b3c36c259b4
(https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft/pull/2974), MIT OR Apache-2.0;
crates/pdf/tests/knockout.rs adapts its test.
The reader retains the existing Foxit/PDFium standard-14 font assets (BSD-3-Clause),
Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram (CC0-1.0), and dependency CMaps (BSD-3-Clause).
Their licences and per-file hashes are retained in vendor/hayro-interpret/assets,
vendor/hayro-cmap/assets, licenses/pdf-backend and ATTRIBUTION.md. These are existing reader dependency assets;
applications retain their own document-font selection and licensing policy.

The Canon CRW decoder in crates/crw (src/lib.rs, src/tables.rs, src/tests.rs, tests/) and its
derivation record (crates/crw/clean-room/) come from:
- LightCraft, crates/raw/src/vendor/crw.rs, crates/raw/src/vendor/crw_tables.rs,
  crates/raw/tests/crw_oracle.rs, crates/raw/tests/it/vendor_robust.rs and clean-room/crw/,
  https://github.com/storytold/lightcraft/pull/3021, revision 6ca439b5155820d2befeacd81ec0c6a80b2bd3bc
  (decoder ported at 70c755e42a65661401142819278d51b0c5977f7f; the record is identical at both).
  https://github.com/storytold/lightcraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the LightCraft contributors.
Its white-level plateau estimate (saturation_plateau) is LightCraft's vendor::white_from_data at the
same revision. The CRW compression was derived clean-room from CC0 sample files and the
maintainer's own photographs by black-box comparison with a reference decoder's command-line
output; no raw-decoder source was consulted (crates/crw/clean-room/sources.md).


https://github.com/storytold/craft-libs/tree/89fa1559e94378ecef85cd7e4681400f4df053e0

Optional PDF writer

Krilla 0.8.2 (https://github.com/LaurenzV/krilla), MIT OR Apache-2.0.
Upstream licence texts are retained in licenses/pdf-writer.
The optional writer embeds Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram
(https://github.com/saucecontrol/Compact-ICC-Profiles), under CC0-1.0.
See licenses/pdf-writer/LICENSE-ICC.txt and ATTRIBUTION.md for sources and hashes.
The writer adds no font files. Its four existing ICC profiles are retained under vendor/krilla/icc.

Krilla is vendored with the local patches in vendor/krilla/patches; see vendor/krilla/README.craft.md
and vendor/krilla/SOURCE.json. Its upstream MIT/Apache-2.0 licences and CC0 profiles are unchanged.

Optional PDF reader

Hayro 0.7.1, hayro-interpret 0.7.0, hayro-syntax 0.7.2, hayro-jbig2 0.3.0, hayro-cmap 0.1.0
and hayro-postscript 0.1.0 (https://github.com/LaurenzV/hayro), MIT OR Apache-2.0, retain their
upstream licence files. The first five are patched sources transferred from PdfCraft
(https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft): hayro-cmap from revision
a2ec27b4775f53c40cad3456936d9406611e3a8d, the others from revision
ec0a965074f376a5bf610435948e4e5184bf3299. hayro-postscript is the crates.io 0.1.0 package
(upstream revision a2354aebaf86f5ab13e5fd6b6b0521306488bf9d) with one local patch.
See vendor/README.craft-reader.md and each crate's SOURCE.json for provenance and local changes.
The hayro-interpret plate mode (separations, overprint and output-intent simulation) is ported
from PdfCraft pull request #2770 by Guillem Hermida, commit
6fcf2a4aca29e943e28cb69017ff8f87d00656c6 (https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft/pull/2770),
MIT OR Apache-2.0; crates/pdf/tests/plates.rs and tests/support/synthetic_icc.rs adapt its tests.
Knockout transparency groups in hayro-interpret and hayro are ported from PdfCraft pull request
#2974 by mtlcl410, commit bae69819594a6385a027fec1f67e6b3c36c259b4
(https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft/pull/2974), MIT OR Apache-2.0;
crates/pdf/tests/knockout.rs adapts its test.
The reader retains the existing Foxit/PDFium standard-14 font assets (BSD-3-Clause),
Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram (CC0-1.0), and dependency CMaps (BSD-3-Clause).
Their licences and per-file hashes are retained in vendor/hayro-interpret/assets,
vendor/hayro-cmap/assets, licenses/pdf-backend and ATTRIBUTION.md. These are existing reader dependency assets;
applications retain their own document-font selection and licensing policy.

The Canon CRW decoder in crates/crw (src/lib.rs, src/tables.rs, src/tests.rs, tests/) and its
derivation record (crates/crw/clean-room/) come from:
- LightCraft, crates/raw/src/vendor/crw.rs, crates/raw/src/vendor/crw_tables.rs,
  crates/raw/tests/crw_oracle.rs, crates/raw/tests/it/vendor_robust.rs and clean-room/crw/,
  https://github.com/storytold/lightcraft/pull/3021, revision 6ca439b5155820d2befeacd81ec0c6a80b2bd3bc
  (decoder ported at 70c755e42a65661401142819278d51b0c5977f7f; the record is identical at both).
  https://github.com/storytold/lightcraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the LightCraft contributors.
Its white-level plateau estimate (saturation_plateau) is LightCraft's vendor::white_from_data at the
same revision. The CRW compression was derived clean-room from CC0 sample files and the
maintainer's own photographs by black-box comparison with a reference decoder's command-line
output; no raw-decoder source was consulted (crates/crw/clean-room/sources.md).

- VectorCraft, crates/ui-egui/src/icons.rs, revision 825d4fdb8b527c597855d273fb4bcc1971855b6a.
  https://github.com/storytold/vectorcraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the VectorCraft contributors.
The icon-button interaction/style boundary is adapted from PhotoCraft (above) and:
- PdfCraft, crates/ui-egui/src/icons.rs, revision f626db25cca2c89f8fa3f7e30d22bf59faea65ec.

The pane sizing and resize boundaries in crates/ui/src/layout draw on:

crates/ui-egui/src/theme.rs, and:

The transactional panel model and drop-zone semantics in crates/ui/src/docking draw on the
FilmCraft/EffectCraft split, tab and accordion layouts and VectorCraft floating groups listed
above. The generic transaction validation, optional renderer and workspace-location helpers
are implemented here without dependencies on application engines or native window helpers.
The hyphenation code, data and generator in crates/text (src/hyphen/, assets/hyphen/ and
examples/hyphgen.rs) are adapted from:

  Licensed MIT OR Apache-2.0.

Optional PDF writer

Krilla 0.8.2 (https://github.com/LaurenzV/krilla), MIT OR Apache-2.0.
Upstream licence texts are retained in licenses/pdf-writer.
The optional writer embeds Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram
(https://github.com/saucecontrol/Compact-ICC-Profiles), under CC0-1.0.
See licenses/pdf-writer/LICENSE-ICC.txt and ATTRIBUTION.md for sources and hashes.
The writer adds no font files. Its four existing ICC profiles are retained under vendor/krilla/icc.

Krilla is vendored with the local patches in vendor/krilla/patches; see vendor/krilla/README.craft.md
and vendor/krilla/SOURCE.json. Its upstream MIT/Apache-2.0 licences and CC0 profiles are unchanged.

Optional PDF reader

Hayro 0.7.1, hayro-interpret 0.7.0, hayro-syntax 0.7.2, hayro-jbig2 0.3.0, hayro-cmap 0.1.0
and hayro-postscript 0.1.0 (https://github.com/LaurenzV/hayro), MIT OR Apache-2.0, retain their
upstream licence files. The first five are patched sources transferred from PdfCraft
(https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft): hayro-cmap from revision
a2ec27b4775f53c40cad3456936d9406611e3a8d, the others from revision
ec0a965074f376a5bf610435948e4e5184bf3299. hayro-postscript is the crates.io 0.1.0 package
(upstream revision a2354aebaf86f5ab13e5fd6b6b0521306488bf9d) with one local patch.
See vendor/README.craft-reader.md and each crate's SOURCE.json for provenance and local changes.
The hayro-interpret plate mode (separations, overprint and output-intent simulation) is ported
from PdfCraft pull request #2770 by Guillem Hermida, commit
6fcf2a4aca29e943e28cb69017ff8f87d00656c6 (https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft/pull/2770),
MIT OR Apache-2.0; crates/pdf/tests/plates.rs and tests/support/synthetic_icc.rs adapt its tests.
Knockout transparency groups in hayro-interpret and hayro are ported from PdfCraft pull request
#2974 by mtlcl410, commit bae69819594a6385a027fec1f67e6b3c36c259b4
(https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft/pull/2974), MIT OR Apache-2.0;
crates/pdf/tests/knockout.rs adapts its test.
The reader retains the existing Foxit/PDFium standard-14 font assets (BSD-3-Clause),
Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram (CC0-1.0), and dependency CMaps (BSD-3-Clause).
Their licences and per-file hashes are retained in vendor/hayro-interpret/assets,
vendor/hayro-cmap/assets, licenses/pdf-backend and ATTRIBUTION.md. These are existing reader dependency assets;
applications retain their own document-font selection and licensing policy.

  0c72d95425dece90ef9a1cceb49e3315c96e22d5, 794a25fc9ed2e3f6fab51a254625bc9b99a88015

The Canon CRW decoder in crates/crw (src/lib.rs, src/tables.rs, src/tests.rs, tests/) and its
derivation record (crates/crw/clean-room/) come from:
- LightCraft, crates/raw/src/vendor/crw.rs, crates/raw/src/vendor/crw_tables.rs,
  crates/raw/tests/crw_oracle.rs, crates/raw/tests/it/vendor_robust.rs and clean-room/crw/,
  https://github.com/storytold/lightcraft/pull/3021, revision 6ca439b5155820d2befeacd81ec0c6a80b2bd3bc
  (decoder ported at 70c755e42a65661401142819278d51b0c5977f7f; the record is identical at both).
  https://github.com/storytold/lightcraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the LightCraft contributors.
Its white-level plateau estimate (saturation_plateau) is LightCraft's vendor::white_from_data at the
same revision. The CRW compression was derived clean-room from CC0 sample files and the
maintainer's own photographs by black-box comparison with a reference decoder's command-line
output; no raw-decoder source was consulted (crates/crw/clean-room/sources.md).


https://github.com/storytold/craft-libs/tree/ca1b5e2edb8568335c6c14f390307bd5beae8238

  exception is Slovak (hyph-sk.tex, Copyright (C) 1992 Jana Chlebíková), under the GNU General
  Public License, version 2 or later (SPDX: GPL-2.0-or-later). craft-text asks for it through the
  feature "slovak-gpl", which "all-languages" does not include; an application that enables it
  ships GPL-licensed data. Cargo unifies features, so another dependency that enables hypher's
  "slovak" feature (hypher's default features do) embeds the same data; a build that must exclude
  it checks its dependency graph. The Spanish patterns (feature "spanish") are hyph-es.tex 5.0.

crates/ui-egui/src/theme.rs, and:
- DesignCraft, crates/ui-egui/src/theme.rs, revision 66c7ce7e96ec286a091878cc2541ee8369aa79d6.
  https://github.com/storytold/designcraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the DesignCraft contributors.
The transactional panel model and drop-zone semantics in crates/ui/src/docking draw on the
FilmCraft/EffectCraft split, tab and accordion layouts and VectorCraft floating groups listed
above. The generic transaction validation, optional renderer and workspace-location helpers
are implemented here without dependencies on application engines or native window helpers.
The hyphenation code, data and generator in crates/text (src/hyphen/, assets/hyphen/ and
examples/hyphgen.rs) are adapted from:

  https://github.com/storytold/wordcraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the WordCraft contributors.
All sources listed above are licensed MIT OR Apache-2.0. No icon artwork is copied here.

Optional PDF writer

Krilla 0.8.2 (https://github.com/LaurenzV/krilla), MIT OR Apache-2.0.
Upstream licence texts are retained in licenses/pdf-writer.
The optional writer embeds Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram
(https://github.com/saucecontrol/Compact-ICC-Profiles), under CC0-1.0.
See licenses/pdf-writer/LICENSE-ICC.txt and ATTRIBUTION.md for sources and hashes.
The writer adds no font files. Its four existing ICC profiles are retained under vendor/krilla/icc.

Krilla is vendored with the local patches in vendor/krilla/patches; see vendor/krilla/README.craft.md
and vendor/krilla/SOURCE.json. Its upstream MIT/Apache-2.0 licences and CC0 profiles are unchanged.

Optional PDF reader

Hayro 0.7.1, hayro-interpret 0.7.0, hayro-syntax 0.7.2, hayro-jbig2 0.3.0, hayro-cmap 0.1.0
and hayro-postscript 0.1.0 (https://github.com/LaurenzV/hayro), MIT OR Apache-2.0, retain their
upstream licence files. The first five are patched sources transferred from PdfCraft
(https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft): hayro-cmap from revision
a2ec27b4775f53c40cad3456936d9406611e3a8d, the others from revision
ec0a965074f376a5bf610435948e4e5184bf3299. hayro-postscript is the crates.io 0.1.0 package
(upstream revision a2354aebaf86f5ab13e5fd6b6b0521306488bf9d) with one local patch.
See vendor/README.craft-reader.md and each crate's SOURCE.json for provenance and local changes.
The hayro-interpret plate mode (separations, overprint and output-intent simulation) is ported
from PdfCraft pull request #2770 by Guillem Hermida, commit
6fcf2a4aca29e943e28cb69017ff8f87d00656c6 (https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft/pull/2770),
MIT OR Apache-2.0; crates/pdf/tests/plates.rs and tests/support/synthetic_icc.rs adapt its tests.
Knockout transparency groups in hayro-interpret and hayro are ported from PdfCraft pull request
#2974 by mtlcl410, commit bae69819594a6385a027fec1f67e6b3c36c259b4
(https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft/pull/2974), MIT OR Apache-2.0;
crates/pdf/tests/knockout.rs adapts its test.
The reader retains the existing Foxit/PDFium standard-14 font assets (BSD-3-Clause),
Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram (CC0-1.0), and dependency CMaps (BSD-3-Clause).
Their licences and per-file hashes are retained in vendor/hayro-interpret/assets,
vendor/hayro-cmap/assets, licenses/pdf-backend and ATTRIBUTION.md. These are existing reader dependency assets;
applications retain their own document-font selection and licensing policy.

The Canon CRW decoder in crates/crw (src/lib.rs, src/tables.rs, src/tests.rs, tests/) and its
derivation record (crates/crw/clean-room/) come from:
- LightCraft, crates/raw/src/vendor/crw.rs, crates/raw/src/vendor/crw_tables.rs,
  crates/raw/tests/crw_oracle.rs, crates/raw/tests/it/vendor_robust.rs and clean-room/crw/,
  https://github.com/storytold/lightcraft/pull/3021, revision 6ca439b5155820d2befeacd81ec0c6a80b2bd3bc
  (decoder ported at 70c755e42a65661401142819278d51b0c5977f7f; the record is identical at both).
  https://github.com/storytold/lightcraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the LightCraft contributors.
Its white-level plateau estimate (saturation_plateau) is LightCraft's vendor::white_from_data at the
same revision. The CRW compression was derived clean-room from CC0 sample files and the
maintainer's own photographs by black-box comparison with a reference decoder's command-line
output; no raw-decoder source was consulted (crates/crw/clean-room/sources.md).

The PDF spooler in crates/print is extracted from:

  revision 33c14e07cfd742a82bf03baa6b103c01008fa68c.
  https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the PdfCraft contributors.
  Licensed MIT OR Apache-2.0. PDF composition and application code are not copied.

The Windows printer duplex-capability query and parser additionally draw on PdfCraft,
crates/print/src/spool.rs, revision fb97ce958dfe92b6762ce40615c7836551710139 (PR #1154), under the same licenses above.

The native Windows backend (crates/print/src/windows.rs, ticket.rs and the printer-list and
Windows spooler tests) is ported from PdfCraft's native printing work, which carries the
approach of PdfCraft PR #599 (Win32 printer list, Windows.Data.Pdf into the XPS print API, the
PrintTicket and its driver-paper preference):
- PdfCraft, crates/print/src/spool/windows.rs, spool.rs and tests.rs, branch
  claude/native-windows-print-826c99 of PR #1132, revision a435117 (jcl-bit/printcraft).
  https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the PdfCraft contributors.
  Licensed MIT OR Apache-2.0.

The menu layout and native-menu driver in crates/menu are extracted from:
- PhotoCraft, crates/ui-egui/src/native_menu.rs, revision 6f85054288a564f9fbbccb99268ffdb34e581584.
  https://github.com/storytold/photocraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the PhotoCraft contributors.
  Licensed MIT OR Apache-2.0. PhotoCraft's menus, translations and command handling are not copied.
The macOS backend in crates/menu/src/mac.rs is adapted from:
- PhotoCraft, apps/photocraft/src/mac_menu.rs, revision 6f85054288a564f9fbbccb99268ffdb34e581584.
  https://github.com/storytold/photocraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the PhotoCraft contributors.
- VectorCraft, apps/vectorcraft/src/mac_menu.rs, revision 0d0ea352df99044589a7ce0efc7004f7a5b6b7c0
  (closing an open menu on a click or Escape, and its tests, in crates/menu/src/driver.rs).
  https://github.com/storytold/vectorcraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the VectorCraft contributors.
  Both licensed MIT OR Apache-2.0.
The egui menu bar in crates/menu/src/bar (its menu navigation, scrolling, submenu hover
intent, keyboard access and mnemonics, and their tests) is ported from:
- PhotoCraft, crates/ui-egui/src/menu_nav.rs and menu_nav_tests.rs, menu_keys.rs and
  menu_keys_tests.rs, and the menu bar in crates/ui-egui/src/menus.rs, revision
  c36e46c3873ee39f299cc50a3dcd28494c78366d.
  https://github.com/storytold/photocraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the PhotoCraft contributors, among them aiongg, artman67,
  BFlat, Brandon Thomas, Brennan Stehling, Daniel Lošák, David Mann, Fabio Lissi, FredApps,
  Higgs32584, itsF4LCON, jason4short, Korg1ch, MarekSpodBiedry and Nigel Ihza Firdausy.
  Licensed MIT OR Apache-2.0. PhotoCraft's menus, theme and Help search are not copied.

The colour management module in crates/cms is extracted from:
- PhotoCraft, crates/cms, revision b3222ee8e7e6bcb4799c37f904c595f20f9baf5e.
  https://github.com/storytold/photocraft

  (CC0-1.0); see ATTRIBUTION.md.

Optional PDF writer

Krilla 0.8.2 (https://github.com/LaurenzV/krilla), MIT OR Apache-2.0.
Upstream licence texts are retained in licenses/pdf-writer.
The optional writer embeds Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram
(https://github.com/saucecontrol/Compact-ICC-Profiles), under CC0-1.0.
See licenses/pdf-writer/LICENSE-ICC.txt and ATTRIBUTION.md for sources and hashes.
The writer adds no font files. Its four existing ICC profiles are retained under vendor/krilla/icc.

Krilla is vendored with the local patches in vendor/krilla/patches; see vendor/krilla/README.craft.md
and vendor/krilla/SOURCE.json. Its upstream MIT/Apache-2.0 licences and CC0 profiles are unchanged.

Optional PDF reader

Hayro 0.7.1, hayro-interpret 0.7.0, hayro-syntax 0.7.2, hayro-jbig2 0.3.0, hayro-cmap 0.1.0
and hayro-postscript 0.1.0 (https://github.com/LaurenzV/hayro), MIT OR Apache-2.0, retain their
upstream licence files. The first five are patched sources transferred from PdfCraft
(https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft): hayro-cmap from revision
a2ec27b4775f53c40cad3456936d9406611e3a8d, the others from revision
ec0a965074f376a5bf610435948e4e5184bf3299. hayro-postscript is the crates.io 0.1.0 package
(upstream revision a2354aebaf86f5ab13e5fd6b6b0521306488bf9d) with one local patch.
See vendor/README.craft-reader.md and each crate's SOURCE.json for provenance and local changes.
The hayro-interpret plate mode (separations, overprint and output-intent simulation) is ported
from PdfCraft pull request #2770 by Guillem Hermida, commit
6fcf2a4aca29e943e28cb69017ff8f87d00656c6 (https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft/pull/2770),
MIT OR Apache-2.0; crates/pdf/tests/plates.rs and tests/support/synthetic_icc.rs adapt its tests.
Knockout transparency groups in hayro-interpret and hayro are ported from PdfCraft pull request
#2974 by mtlcl410, commit bae69819594a6385a027fec1f67e6b3c36c259b4
(https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft/pull/2974), MIT OR Apache-2.0;
crates/pdf/tests/knockout.rs adapts its test.
The reader retains the existing Foxit/PDFium standard-14 font assets (BSD-3-Clause),
Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram (CC0-1.0), and dependency CMaps (BSD-3-Clause).
Their licences and per-file hashes are retained in vendor/hayro-interpret/assets,
vendor/hayro-cmap/assets, licenses/pdf-backend and ATTRIBUTION.md. These are existing reader dependency assets;
applications retain their own document-font selection and licensing policy.

Platform lifecycle source provenance

The optional crates/platform services adapt the preserved local platform candidate
9b135779a1e7136438a217c18afe1693ea23f1ab (dialog outcomes and browser resource ownership),
and current application implementations:
- PdfCraft crates/platform/src/desktop_theme.rs, a8cd797a917b1007544575c7280c9a0ce6a17e8f.
- CADCraft apps/cadcraft/src/desktop_theme.rs, 0cc66ad3066e71f2c3d4cacb7de905e79ba1c95b.
- ArtCraft Launcher apps/artcraft-launcher/src/system_theme.rs, f1676dfb41c226b5be490721f486ee6427419d80.
- Earlier PhotoCraft, FilmCraft and EffectCraft appearance services at revisions
  0c72d95425dece90ef9a1cceb49e3315c96e22d5, 794a25fc9ed2e3f6fab51a254625bc9b99a88015
  and 1b4d47f34f2b1c1b4a89f4b7348889def8fd648b respectively.
Sources: https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft, https://github.com/storytold/cadcraft,
https://github.com/storytold/craft-launcher, https://github.com/storytold/photocraft,
https://github.com/storytold/filmcraft, https://github.com/storytold/effectcraft.
Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the respective application and craft-libs contributors.
All adapted source is licensed MIT OR Apache-2.0.

The Canon CRW decoder in crates/crw (src/lib.rs, src/tables.rs, src/tests.rs, tests/) and its
derivation record (crates/crw/clean-room/) come from:
- LightCraft, crates/raw/src/vendor/crw.rs, crates/raw/src/vendor/crw_tables.rs,
  crates/raw/tests/crw_oracle.rs, crates/raw/tests/it/vendor_robust.rs and clean-room/crw/,
  https://github.com/storytold/lightcraft/pull/3021, revision 6ca439b5155820d2befeacd81ec0c6a80b2bd3bc
  (decoder ported at 70c755e42a65661401142819278d51b0c5977f7f; the record is identical at both).
  https://github.com/storytold/lightcraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the LightCraft contributors.
Its white-level plateau estimate (saturation_plateau) is LightCraft's vendor::white_from_data at the
same revision. The CRW compression was derived clean-room from CC0 sample files and the
maintainer's own photographs by black-box comparison with a reference decoder's command-line
output; no raw-decoder source was consulted (crates/crw/clean-room/sources.md).


GridCraft PDF metrics attribution — metrics.rs
 Advance widths of the standard 14 PDF fonts, in 1/1000 em, for the printable ASCII range.

 Source: the glyph advance widths ("WX" values) of Adobe's Core 14 AFM font metrics files
 (Helvetica, Helvetica-Bold, Times-Roman, Times-Bold, Courier). Adobe distributes those metrics
 files with permission to use, copy and distribute them for any purpose ("This file and the 14
 PostScript(R) AFM files it accompanies may be used, copied, and distributed for any purpose and
 without charge, with or without modification, provided that all copyright notices are retained;
 that the AFM files are not distributed without this file; that all modifications to this file
 or any of the AFM files are prominently noted in the modified file(s); and that this paragraph
 is not modified."). Only the numeric widths are reproduced here, typed in from the public
 metrics; no AFM file is included. Oblique/italic faces reuse the upright widths (an
 approximation that is exact for Helvetica and close for Times), and characters outside ASCII
 are approximated by their base letter.


GridCraft PDF metrics attribution — symbol.rs
 The standard Symbol font: its built-in encoding and advance widths.

 Source: the character codes and glyph advance widths ("C" and "WX" values) of Adobe's Core 14
 AFM metrics file for Symbol (`Symbol.afm`, version 001.008), and the Unicode values of its glyph
 names from Unicode, Inc.'s `symbol.txt` ("Adobe Symbol Encoding to Unicode", table version
 1.0). Adobe distributes the Core 14 metrics with permission to use, copy and distribute them
 for any purpose (the notice is quoted in `metrics.rs`); Unicode, Inc. grants the right to use
 the information in its file in products supporting the Unicode Standard (its notice is
 recorded in `ATTRIBUTION.md`). Only the numbers are reproduced here, typed in from the public
 tables; neither file is included. The stretchable
 bracket, brace and integral pieces (codes 0xE6 to 0xEF and 0xF3 to 0xFE) and the sans-serif
 duplicates of the registered, copyright and trademark signs are left out of the Unicode map:
 they have no Unicode counterpart of their own.


GridCraft Symbol mapping notice from ATTRIBUTION.md:
Unicode, Inc. hereby grants the right to freely use the information supplied in this file in the creation of products supporting the Unicode Standard, and to make copies of this file in any form for internal or external distribution as long as this notice remains attached. The file is provided as-is, without warranties.

Optional PDF reader

Hayro 0.7.1, hayro-interpret 0.7.0, hayro-syntax 0.7.2, hayro-jbig2 0.3.0, hayro-cmap 0.1.0
and hayro-postscript 0.1.0 (https://github.com/LaurenzV/hayro), MIT OR Apache-2.0, retain their
upstream licence files. The first five are patched sources transferred from PdfCraft
(https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft): hayro-cmap from revision
a2ec27b4775f53c40cad3456936d9406611e3a8d, the others from revision
ec0a965074f376a5bf610435948e4e5184bf3299. hayro-postscript is the crates.io 0.1.0 package
(upstream revision a2354aebaf86f5ab13e5fd6b6b0521306488bf9d) with one local patch.
See vendor/README.craft-reader.md and each crate's SOURCE.json for provenance and local changes.
The hayro-interpret plate mode (separations, overprint and output-intent simulation) is ported
from PdfCraft pull request #2770 by Guillem Hermida, commit
6fcf2a4aca29e943e28cb69017ff8f87d00656c6 (https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft/pull/2770),
MIT OR Apache-2.0; crates/pdf/tests/plates.rs and tests/support/synthetic_icc.rs adapt its tests.
Knockout transparency groups in hayro-interpret and hayro are ported from PdfCraft pull request
#2974 by mtlcl410, commit bae69819594a6385a027fec1f67e6b3c36c259b4
(https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft/pull/2974), MIT OR Apache-2.0;
crates/pdf/tests/knockout.rs adapts its test.
The reader retains the existing Foxit/PDFium standard-14 font assets (BSD-3-Clause),
Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram (CC0-1.0), and dependency CMaps (BSD-3-Clause).
Their licences and per-file hashes are retained in vendor/hayro-interpret/assets,
vendor/hayro-cmap/assets, licenses/pdf-backend and ATTRIBUTION.md. These are existing reader dependency assets;
applications retain their own document-font selection and licensing policy.

  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the LightCraft contributors.
Its white-level plateau estimate (saturation_plateau) is LightCraft's vendor::white_from_data at the
same revision. The CRW compression was derived clean-room from CC0 sample files and the
maintainer's own photographs by black-box comparison with a reference decoder's command-line
output; no raw-decoder source was consulted (crates/crw/clean-room/sources.md).

crates/audio/src/alac/ (the ALAC decoder and encoder) is ported from Apple's ALAC reference
implementation, https://github.com/macosforge/alac at c38887c5c5e64a4b31108733bd79ca9b2496d987
(codec/), Copyright (c) 2011 Apple Inc., licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0
(LICENSE-APACHE). The files are modified: translated to Rust, with bounds checks on hostile input,
wrapping integer arithmetic, the unrolled predictor and per-width matrix routines folded into one,
and the average bit rate filled in. Those files are Apache-2.0 only.

## Assets embedded by the optional PDF writer

Krilla 0.8.2 bundles the following Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram, under CC0-1.0.
The immutable source revision is `3ffdf0588cf98050aad6edba51ca70162e1fb5b5`, recorded by the
registry package's `.cargo_vcs_info.json`. These are the same dependency profiles already
used by the apps' Krilla exporters. The local backend snapshot retains these four profiles; the optional writer adds no font binaries.

## Assets embedded by the optional PDF writer

Krilla 0.8.2 bundles the following Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram, under CC0-1.0.
The immutable source revision is `3ffdf0588cf98050aad6edba51ca70162e1fb5b5`, recorded by the
registry package's `.cargo_vcs_info.json`. These are the same dependency profiles already
used by the apps' Krilla exporters. The local backend snapshot retains these four profiles; the optional writer adds no font binaries.
The profile licence is retained in `licenses/pdf-writer/LICENSE-ICC.txt`.

patched crates.io `hayro-postscript` 0.1.0 scanner, which bundles no assets.

in `vendor/hayro-interpret`, and the CMaps in `vendor/hayro-cmap/assets`; no application
document fonts are added. The removed upstream LAB.icc profile is not restored. Source hashes

and vendor/krilla/SOURCE.json. Its upstream MIT/Apache-2.0 licences and CC0 profiles are unchanged.

Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram (CC0-1.0), and dependency CMaps (BSD-3-Clause).
Their licences and per-file hashes are retained in vendor/hayro-interpret/assets,
licenses/pdf-backend and ATTRIBUTION.md. These are existing reader dependency assets;
applications retain their own document-font selection and licensing policy.

## Assets embedded by the optional PDF writer

Krilla 0.8.2 bundles the following Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram, under CC0-1.0.
The immutable source revision is `3ffdf0588cf98050aad6edba51ca70162e1fb5b5`, recorded by the
registry package's `.cargo_vcs_info.json`. These are the same dependency profiles already
used by the apps' Krilla exporters. The local backend snapshot retains these four profiles; the optional writer adds no font binaries.
The profile licence is retained in `licenses/pdf-writer/LICENSE-ICC.txt`.

| Crate / file | Author | Source | Licence | SHA-256 | Use |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

| `krilla 0.8.2 / icc/sGrey-v4.icc` | Clinton Ingram | https://github.com/LaurenzV/krilla/blob/3ffdf0588cf98050aad6edba51ca70162e1fb5b5/crates/krilla/icc/sGrey-v4.icc | CC0-1.0 | `00c0f94e09127520a17dc0e1d9264b5702081d96dbdb1549ee88e3631ce42a9d` | PDF writer colour profiles |

## Assets retained with the optional PDF reader

The shared reader centralizes the five patched Hayro source trees from PdfCraft and the
patched crates.io `hayro-postscript` 0.1.0 scanner, which bundles no assets.
The standard-14 fonts, CMYK profile and generated metrics listed below remain byte-identical
in `vendor/hayro-interpret`, and the CMaps in `vendor/hayro-cmap/assets`; no application
document fonts are added. The removed upstream LAB.icc profile is not restored. Source hashes
and the exact transfer revision are recorded in each vendored crate's SOURCE.json.

Every non-code asset in FilmCraft, with its author, source and licence. Each file listed here also has
a `<file>.attribution` sidecar with full details. The rules are in [AGENTS.md](AGENTS.md) §1: no Adobe
iconography, images or other Adobe assets; open licences only; every asset attributed.

procedural demo footage (`crates/media/src/generators.rs`); no external media. Regenerate with

## Assets drawn or generated in code

These are original work by FilmCraft contributors under the project licence (MIT OR Apache-2.0). None
is traced or derived from Adobe artwork.

| Asset | Where |
|---|---|
| UI icons (tools, transport, panels, header) | `crates/ui-egui/src/icons.rs`: vector paths on a 16×16 grid |
| Source and Program range braces and trim hover cues | `crates/ui-egui/src/panels/monitor.rs` and `source_range.rs`: original generic vector shapes by FilmCraft contributors, MIT OR Apache-2.0; no third-party artwork |
| Align / distribute / paragraph-alignment glyphs in the graphics panel | `crates/ui-egui/src/panels/graphics.rs` (`align_glyph`): bars and lines drawn with the egui painter |
| Demo footage (ocean sunset, aurora, city night, dunes, forest, plasma), bars and tone, counting leader, colour matte | `crates/media/src/generators.rs` |
| Built-in graphics templates (Lower Third – Slab / Rule, Ticker – Crawl, Title – Centered / Boxed, End Credits – Roll, Callout – Pointer / Tag) and their placeholder text | `crates/project/src/gtemplate.rs` (`builtin_templates`): original designs defined in code with the bundled Inter font; thumbnails are rendered at runtime, no template, image or font files |
| Lumetri "Look" presets (Teal & Orange, Warm Film, …) | `crates/render/src/effects.rs` (`apply_look`): procedural colour transforms, not LUT files |
| Lumetri Presets (Effects panel: Cinematic, Film Emulation, Monochrome, Technical) and their preview picture | `crates/render/src/lumetri_presets.rs`: Lumetri parameter values chosen by contributors, descriptive names, and a procedural preview picture (dusk sky, ridges, lake, grey ramp, colour chips) |
| Colour palette and layout metrics | `crates/ui-egui/src/theme.rs`: colour values and sizes only; no artwork |

## Optional build input: craft-fonts

These fonts are **not files in this repository**. Builds made with the optional build input
`CRAFT_FONTS_DIR` (all official releases; see [docs/contributing.md](docs/contributing.md#building-with-craft-fonts))
embed them from [storytold/craft-fonts](https://github.com/storytold/craft-fonts), where each file,
its author, pinned upstream source and licence text are listed in its
[ATTRIBUTION.md](https://github.com/storytold/craft-fonts/blob/main/ATTRIBUTION.md). Release packages
carry each font's licence as `OFL-<family>.txt`.

| Font | Use in FilmCraft | Licence |
|---|---|---|
| BIZ UDPGothic Regular, Bold (Morisawa) | Japanese UI text; Japanese fallback for sans titles (the web build embeds only Regular) | OFL-1.1 |
| Shippori Mincho Regular (FONTDASU) | Japanese fallback for serif titles (native builds) | OFL-1.1 |
| BIZ UDMincho Regular (Morisawa) | Japanese fallback for serif titles (native builds) | OFL-1.1 |
| Noto Sans CJK SC Regular (Google, Adobe) | Chinese UI text and media names; Chinese fallback for titles (native builds) | OFL-1.1 |
| Noto Sans Arabic Regular (Google) | Arabic UI text; Arabic fallback for titles (native builds) | OFL-1.1 |
| Noto Sans Thai Regular (Google) | Thai fallback for titles (native builds) | OFL-1.1 |
| Noto Sans Georgian Regular (Google) | Georgian fallback for titles (native builds) | OFL-1.1 |

## Third-party source code

Source files that are transcribed from third-party code rather than written from scratch. Each keeps
the original copyright and permission notice in its header.

| File | Author | Source | Licence |
|---|---|---|---|
| `crates/platform/src/nvenc/ffi.rs` | NVIDIA Corporation (`nvEncodeAPI.h`); FilmCraft contributors (Rust transcription) | Structures (including `NV_ENC_CONFIG_HEVC` and its bit-field masks, `NV_ENC_PIC_PARAMS_HEVC`, `NV_ENC_SEI_PAYLOAD`, `NV_ENC_TIME_CODE`), function table, codec / profile GUIDs (H.264, HEVC Main, HEVC Main 10) and constants (`NV_ENC_LEVEL_HEVC_*`, tier, CU size, `NV_ENC_BUFFER_FORMAT_YUV420_10BIT`, `NV_ENC_CAPS_SUPPORT_10BIT_ENCODE`, BT.2020 / PQ / HLG VUI codes) transcribed from `nvEncodeAPI.h`, NVIDIA Video Codec SDK, API 12.1 (https://developer.nvidia.com/video-codec-sdk) | MIT (Copyright (c) 2010-2023 NVIDIA Corporation; notice kept in the file header) |

## Downloaded at runtime

These files are never in the repository or the app bundle. FilmCraft downloads them into the
per-user data directory (`<data dir>/models/<id>/`) only after the user confirms a dialog that shows
the size, the source URL and the licence. Every file is pinned to a revision and checked against its
SHA-256 (`crates/speech/src/models.rs`, `docs/transcripts.md`).

| Asset | Author | Source | Licence |
|---|---|---|---|
| Whisper tiny / base / small speech-recognition weights (`model.safetensors`, `config.json`, `generation_config.json`, `tokenizer.json`) | OpenAI | `https://huggingface.co/openai/whisper-{tiny,base,small}` (revisions `169d4a43…`, `e37978b9…`, `973afd24…`); weights released with `https://github.com/openai/whisper` | MIT (weights and code, github.com/openai/whisper); the Hugging Face conversion is labelled Apache-2.0 |

## Assets embedded by the optional PDF writer

Krilla 0.8.2 bundles the following Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram, under CC0-1.0.
The immutable source revision is `3ffdf0588cf98050aad6edba51ca70162e1fb5b5`, recorded by the
registry package's `.cargo_vcs_info.json`. These are the same dependency profiles already
used by the apps' Krilla exporters. The local backend snapshot retains these four profiles; the optional writer adds no font binaries.
The profile licence is retained in `licenses/pdf-writer/LICENSE-ICC.txt`.

| Crate / file | Author | Source | Licence | SHA-256 | Use |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

## Assets retained with the optional PDF reader

The shared reader centralizes the five patched Hayro source trees from PdfCraft and the
patched crates.io `hayro-postscript` 0.1.0 scanner, which bundles no assets.
The standard-14 fonts, CMYK profile and generated metrics listed below remain byte-identical
in `vendor/hayro-interpret`, and the CMaps in `vendor/hayro-cmap/assets`; no application
document fonts are added. The removed upstream LAB.icc profile is not restored. Source hashes
and the exact transfer revision are recorded in each vendored crate's SOURCE.json.

Linux and FreeBSD Wayland window decorations
-------------------------------------------
sctk-adwaita 0.10.1 (https://github.com/PolyMeilex/sctk-adwaita,
commit d5f343c45c04d4a026db57f76760819b7325db00) is used by winit on Linux
and FreeBSD for vector window controls. Its title-font features are disabled: no Cantarell
font is embedded by this dependency and no font file is added to this repo.

Optional PDF reader (craft-pdf)

PhotoCraft imports PDF pages through craft-pdf (https://github.com/storytold/craft-libs,
MIT OR Apache-2.0), pinned to revision c5a1cbfb5f9049f9270955caffb5bee5997aebf7. It vendors
Hayro 0.7.1, hayro-interpret 0.7.0, hayro-syntax 0.7.2, hayro-jbig2 0.3.0 and
hayro-cmap 0.1.0 (https://github.com/LaurenzV/hayro, MIT OR Apache-2.0). The reader bundles
the standard-14 Foxit fonts (BSD-3-Clause), Adobe CMaps (BSD-3-Clause) and a Compact ICC
profile by Clinton Ingram (CC0-1.0); per-file sources and hashes are in craft-libs
ATTRIBUTION.md. No PDF assets are copied into this repository, and PDFium is no longer used or
shipped.

Shared PDF backend

The patched hayro-interpret 0.7.0, hayro-syntax 0.7.2, hayro-jbig2 0.3.0, hayro-cmap 0.1.0
and hayro-postscript 0.1.0 are fetched from https://github.com/storytold/craft-libs at revision
9be9a63782298ab59cd9b7a42ee9cba4f42a5ccb. Their upstream project is
https://github.com/LaurenzV/hayro, licensed MIT OR Apache-2.0. The snapshot retains each
crate's upstream licence files. The patch ledger is at
https://github.com/storytold/craft-libs/blob/9be9a63782298ab59cd9b7a42ee9cba4f42a5ccb/vendor/README.craft-reader.md.

Assets already bundled through Hayro's default features retain their licences:
- Foxit standard-14 substitutes: Copyright 2014 PDFium Authors. All rights reserved.
  Original code copyright 2014 Foxit Software Inc. BSD-3-Clause; see
  licenses/pdf-backend/hayro-interpret/assets/LICENSE_FOXIT.
- CGATS001Compat-v2-micro.icc: CC0-1.0, Clinton Ingram; see
  licenses/pdf-backend/hayro-interpret/assets/CGATS_LICENSE.txt.
- Predefined CMaps: Copyright 1990-2023 Adobe. All rights reserved. BSD-3-Clause,
  non-visual character-mapping data; see licenses/pdf-backend/hayro-cmap/LICENSE.txt.
  Adobe and contributor names do not endorse or promote this product.
Per-file hashes and sources are recorded in ASSETS.md.

Linux Wayland window decorations
-------------------------------
sctk-adwaita 0.10.1 (https://github.com/PolyMeilex/sctk-adwaita,
commit d5f343c45c04d4a026db57f76760819b7325db00) is used by winit on Linux
for vector window controls. Its title-font features are disabled: no Cantarell
font is embedded by this dependency and no font file is added to this repo.

Bundled egui font icons

The pinned egui dependency includes egui-icons.ttf, a small subset of John Slegers'
emoji-icon-font, through default_fonts. The parent emoji-icon-font.ttf face is
included through monochrome_emoji_fonts. Their upstream project is
https://github.com/jslegers/emoji-icon-font; both use the same exact copyright
and permission notice below.

NOTICE
LightCraft
Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the LightCraft contributors.
Licensed under the MIT License or the Apache License, Version 2.0, at your option
(LICENSE-MIT, LICENSE-APACHE).

The ArtCraft name, wordmark and logos in docs/brand/ are trademarks of the ArtCraft Team. They are
not open source and are not covered by the license above; their terms are in
docs/brand/LICENSE-brand.txt.

Third-party material

This product bundles third-party material under its own licenses, listed below. The per-file
list (author, source, license) is in assets/ATTRIBUTION.md.

Inter (assets/fonts/Inter-*.ttf) — SIL Open Font License 1.1. The licence text is
assets/fonts/OFL-Inter.txt in the source tree; release packages ship it beside NOTICE as
OFL-Inter.txt (Windows installers include it in FONT-LICENCES.txt).
Showcase photographs by Ansel Adams, Dorothea Lange and NASA (docs/images/) — public domain. See assets/ATTRIBUTION.md.

crates/raw/src/spectral_table.rs (lightcraft-raw) holds camera colour matrices derived from the
camera spectral sensitivities, training reflectances and model alias list of rawtoaces-data
(https://github.com/AcademySoftwareFoundation/rawtoaces-data, v1.1.0), an Academy Software
Foundation project licensed under the Apache License 2.0, and from the CIE 1931 colour-matching
functions it ships (CIE, CC-BY-4.0). The matrices were fitted by the LightCraft contributors
(2026) with tools/spectral_camera_table.py. See assets/ATTRIBUTION.md.

crates/inpaint (lightcraft-inpaint) ports the inpainting (PatchMatch completion) and Poisson membrane
fill code of PhotoCraft (https://github.com/storytold/photocraft, crates/algo/src/inpaint.rs and
poisson.rs), Copyright the PhotoCraft contributors, licensed under the MIT License or the Apache
License 2.0, at your option. The port was modified by the LightCraft contributors (2026); each
ported file says so.

crates/segment (lightcraft-segment) ports the SAM 3 model code of Hugging Face Transformers
(https://github.com/huggingface/transformers, src/transformers/models/sam3 and sam3_tracker),
Copyright The HuggingFace Team and Meta Platforms, Inc., licensed under the Apache License 2.0.
The port was modified by the LightCraft contributors (2026): translated from Python/PyTorch to
Rust on candle and restructured; each ported file says so. Because of this, crates/segment is
licensed under the Apache License 2.0 only (LICENSE-APACHE), not under the MIT option.

The SAM 3 weights are not part of LightCraft and are never distributed with it. A user downloads
the facebook/sam3 checkpoint only when they choose to (in the app, or by hand), under Meta's SAM
License (https://github.com/facebookresearch/sam3/blob/main/LICENSE), a separate, non-OSI
licence. See docs/ai-masks.md.

AI denoise weights are not part of LightCraft and are never distributed with it.
The default build offers user-supplied models only. The optional rawnind-model
feature offers a separately downloaded RawNIND UtNet2 Bayer checkpoint under
GPL-3.0 (https://www.gnu.org/licenses/gpl-3.0.html), after the user accepts its terms.
Commercial use is subject to those terms, including applicable source, licence and
notice obligations on redistribution. Enabling that offer in releases remains a
maintainer decision. The ONNX reader and CPU/GPU executor are our Rust implementation;
no darktable, RawSpeed, LibRaw or other GPL decoder/runtime code is included.

Builds made with craft-fonts (the optional CRAFT_FONTS_DIR build input; all official releases)
also embed the default fonts listed in
https://github.com/storytold/craft-fonts/blob/b076417b5880bd4154bd22f00695b1d28c6bea83/ATTRIBUTION.md (BIZ UDPGothic, BIZ UDMincho,
Shippori Mincho, Noto Sans CJK SC, Noto Sans Arabic, Noto Sans Thai, Noto Sans Georgian,
NanumGothic, Noto Sans Symbols, Noto Sans Symbols 2, Noto Sans Hebrew and
Noto Sans Devanagari), under the SIL Open Font License 1.1. The opt-in LXGW WenKai Lite face
(fonts/simplified-chinese-manifest.txt in craft-fonts) is not embedded. The web build embeds
BIZ UDPGothic Regular, Noto Sans Thai Regular and Noto Sans Georgian Regular, and loads Noto Sans
CJK SC separately. Release packages include each font's licence as OFL-<family>.txt; Windows
installers include their texts in FONT-LICENCES.txt.

Place names for reverse geocoding (Photo > Fill Location from GPS) are not part of LightCraft and
are never distributed with it. On the user's request (Photo > Download Place Names) the app
downloads cities15000.zip, admin1CodesASCII.txt and countryInfo.txt from GeoNames
(https://download.geonames.org/export/dump/) into the settings folder. GeoNames data is licensed
under Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 (https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/);
attribution: "GeoNames, geonames.org". The lookup code (crates/geocode) is our own.


epaint_default_fonts, the egui default-font crate LightCraft builds with, also bundles Hack Regular
and Ubuntu Light, and Noto Emoji Regular when egui's `monochrome_emoji_fonts` feature is on (the
LightCraft workspace enables both `default_fonts` and `monochrome_emoji_fonts` on egui 0.37). The
LightCraft binaries embed these faces unmodified, registered through egui's default font definitions.
Source for all of them (the same pin as the icon fonts below):
https://github.com/storytold/egui/tree/6d518fc799681571963842f95b55a6b68431b15a/crates/epaint_default_fonts/fonts

  assets/app-icon/LICENSE-document.txt (ISC AND (MIT OR Apache-2.0)), packaged as
  LICENSE-document.txt beside the Windows executables.

  (CRAFT_FONTS_DIR) that is not part of this repository. Builds made with it, which includes every
  official release, embed the fonts listed in
  https://github.com/storytold/craft-fonts/blob/main/ATTRIBUTION.md (BIZ UDPGothic, Shippori Mincho,
  BIZ UDMincho; the web build only BIZ UDPGothic Regular) under the SIL Open Font License 1.1. Those
  packages carry each font's licence as OFL-<family>.txt. Builds made with the craft-fonts revision

  The Noto Project Authors (https://github.com/notofonts/thai), under SIL OFL 1.1; its licence is
  fonts/noto-sans-thai/OFL.txt in the craft-fonts build input.
- hayro (rendering, via crates.io) — Apache-2.0 OR MIT. Its `embed-fonts` feature bundles the Foxit
  standard-14 Type 1 fonts under BSD-3-Clause ("Copyright 2014 PDFium Authors"), and hayro-cmap bundles
  Adobe CMap resources under BSD-3-Clause.
- lopdf (bootstrap document inspection, via crates.io) — MIT.
- egui default fonts (epaint_default_fonts, via eframe) — Ubuntu Light (Ubuntu Font Licence 1.0),

- ICU4X line-breaking data (icu_segmenter_data, via crates.io) — Unicode-3.0. Its Thai, Lao, Khmer
  and Burmese word dictionaries are compiled in for wrapping Thai text; notice below, full licence in
  licenses/ICU4X-LICENSE.txt.

Bundled fonts

EffectCraft embeds Inter, Noto Serif, JetBrains Mono and Noto Sans Symbols 2 from
assets/fonts/. The pinned egui dependency (epaint_default_fonts, revision
6d518fc799681571963842f95b55a6b68431b15a) additionally embeds Ubuntu Light, Hack,
Noto Emoji, egui-icons and emoji-icon-font through EffectCraft's enabled `default_fonts`
and `monochrome_emoji_fonts` features; no font file from that dependency is added to
this repository. The complete copyright notices and licence texts required for all of
these fonts follow.

The optional PDF document reader uses craft-pdf and Hayro-syntax 0.7.2, MIT OR Apache-2.0.
The shared patched syntax source is centralized in storytold/craft-libs, transferred from
PdfCraft revision 9ed3abe4907a127d36b12fbfeee71d7e26248da1. This parser-only use embeds no
Hayro fonts, CMaps or image codecs; EffectCraft keeps its existing fonts and drawing filters.

Linux Wayland window decorations
-------------------------------
sctk-adwaita 0.10.1 (https://github.com/PolyMeilex/sctk-adwaita,
commit d5f343c45c04d4a026db57f76760819b7325db00) is used by winit on Linux
for vector window controls. Its title-font features are disabled: no Cantarell
font is embedded by this dependency and no font file is added to this repo.

- Japanese fonts (craft-fonts): builds made with craft-fonts (CRAFT_FONTS_DIR; all official
  releases) embed the fonts listed in
  https://github.com/storytold/craft-fonts/blob/main/ATTRIBUTION.md (BIZ UDPGothic, BIZ UDMincho,
  Shippori Mincho), under the SIL Open Font License 1.1. Their licence texts ship with the
  packages as OFL-<family>.txt. Builds without CRAFT_FONTS_DIR do not include them.
- All UI icons are drawn in code (crates/ui-egui/src/icons.rs) and are original work, MIT OR Apache-2.0.
- hayro PDF crates (placed PDFs): hayro, hayro-interpret, hayro-syntax and hayro-jbig2
  (Apache-2.0 OR MIT) are fetched from the pinned craft-libs source documented below.
  hayro-interpret embeds PDFium's Foxit standard-14 fonts under PDFium's BSD-style license
  (vendor/hayro-interpret/assets/LICENSE_FOXIT) and a CMYK profile under CC0-1.0.

Rust dependencies (vello_cpu, kurbo, skrifa, harfrust, image, egui, eframe, …) are permissively
licensed (MIT/Apache-2.0/BSD/ISC/Zlib), fetched through crates.io or the pinned source below.

No Adobe icons, images or artwork are included.

PDF backend source

The patched hayro 0.7.1, hayro-interpret 0.7.0, hayro-syntax 0.7.2, hayro-jbig2 0.3.0 and
hayro-cmap 0.1.0 are fetched from https://github.com/storytold/craft-libs at revision
9f75d3dc19b4b514c4f3d4847ed1ec1b0e86b4ef. The first four source snapshots were transferred
from https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft at revision
7437bde378f43856eada40af25b983812e615e8a and hayro-cmap at revision
a2ec27b4775f53c40cad3456936d9406611e3a8d, then patched in craft-libs. Their upstream project is
https://github.com/LaurenzV/hayro, licensed MIT OR Apache-2.0. The snapshot retains each
crate's upstream licence files. The patch ledger is at
https://github.com/storytold/craft-libs/blob/9f75d3dc19b4b514c4f3d4847ed1ec1b0e86b4ef/vendor/README.craft-reader.md.
Per-crate vendor/SOURCE.json files record transferred and local source hashes; asset
origins and unchanged per-file hashes are recorded separately in ASSETS.md.

Assets already bundled through Hayro's default features retain their licences:
- Foxit standard-14 substitutes: Copyright 2014 PDFium Authors. All rights reserved.
  Original code copyright 2014 Foxit Software Inc. BSD-3-Clause; see
  licenses/pdf-backend/hayro-interpret/assets/LICENSE_FOXIT.
- CGATS001Compat-v2-micro.icc: CC0-1.0, Clinton Ingram; see
  licenses/pdf-backend/hayro-interpret/assets/CGATS_LICENSE.txt.
- Predefined CMaps: Copyright 1990-2023 Adobe. All rights reserved. BSD-3-Clause,
  non-visual character-mapping data; see licenses/pdf-backend/hayro-cmap/LICENSE.txt.
  Adobe and contributor names do not endorse or promote this product.
Per-file hashes and sources are recorded in ASSETS.md.

Linux Wayland window decorations
-------------------------------
sctk-adwaita 0.10.1 (https://github.com/PolyMeilex/sctk-adwaita,
commit d5f343c45c04d4a026db57f76760819b7325db00) is used by winit on Linux
for vector window controls. Its title-font features are disabled: no Cantarell
font is embedded by this dependency and no font file is added to this repo.

Bundled egui font icons

The pinned egui dependency includes egui-icons.ttf, a small subset of John Slegers'
emoji-icon-font, through default_fonts. The parent emoji-icon-font.ttf face is
included through monochrome_emoji_fonts. Their upstream project is
https://github.com/jslegers/emoji-icon-font; both use the same exact copyright
and permission notice below.

Third-party material

This product bundles no third-party fonts, icons or images in the repository: every icon is
drawn in code and original to FigCraft. The per-file list (author, source, license) of any bundled
asset is in ATTRIBUTION.md.

Release builds made with the optional craft-fonts build input (CRAFT_FONTS_DIR) embed the fonts
listed in ATTRIBUTION.md (Inter and others, SIL Open Font License 1.1); each package carries their
licence texts.

Rust crate dependencies keep their own licenses (MIT, Apache-2.0, BSD, ISC, Zlib, OFL for egui's
default fonts, CDLA-Permissive-2.0 for the root certificate list webpki-roots compiles in, and
similar permissive licenses); see each crate's metadata.

Linux Wayland window decorations
-------------------------------
sctk-adwaita 0.10.1 (https://github.com/PolyMeilex/sctk-adwaita,
commit d5f343c45c04d4a026db57f76760819b7325db00) is used by winit on Linux
for vector window controls. Its title-font features are disabled: no Cantarell
font is embedded by this dependency and no font file is added to this repo.

egui icon font subset (dependency asset)
----------------------------------------
epaint_default_fonts 0.37 embeds egui-icons.ttf, a five-glyph subset of
emoji-icon-font by John Slegers. The full monochrome emoji-icon-font fallback
uses the same MIT license. Upstream license text follows unchanged.

Third-party material

This product bundles third-party material under its own licenses, listed below. The per-file
list (author, source, license) is in ATTRIBUTION.md.

- Source Sans 3 and Source Serif 4 (assets/fonts/Source*) — SIL Open Font License 1.1. See assets/fonts/OFL-SourceSans3.txt, OFL-SourceSerif4.txt.
- Inter (assets/fonts/Inter-*.ttf) — SIL Open Font License 1.1. See assets/fonts/OFL-Inter.txt.
- JetBrains Mono (assets/fonts/JetBrainsMono-*.ttf) — SIL Open Font License 1.1. See assets/fonts/OFL-JetBrainsMono.txt.
- Japanese fonts (craft-fonts): builds made with craft-fonts (CRAFT_FONTS_DIR; all official
  releases) embed the fonts listed in
  https://github.com/storytold/craft-fonts/blob/main/ATTRIBUTION.md (BIZ UDPGothic, BIZ UDMincho,
  Shippori Mincho), under the SIL Open Font License 1.1. Their licence texts ship with the
  packages as OFL-<family>.txt. Builds without CRAFT_FONTS_DIR do not include them.
- Language data (craft-i18n): the word lists, dictionaries and hyphenation patterns below are embedded at build
  time from https://github.com/storytold/craft-i18n (language-data/, CRAFT_I18N_DIR at the commit
  in craft-i18n.rev; all official releases) by craft-proof (storytold/craft-libs). Builds without
  CRAFT_I18N_DIR do not include them.
- English word list (craft-i18n language-data/en-us/en-us.dic) — derived from the Moby Hyphenator II list by
  Grady Ward, public domain (Project Gutenberg eBook #3204). The hyphenation patterns
  (craft-i18n language-data/en-us/hyphenation.pat) were trained from it and are released as CC0-1.0.
- English spelling dictionaries (craft-i18n language-data/en-us, en-gb and en-ca: spelling.aff and
  spelling.dic) — the SCOWL rel-2026.02.25 Hunspell dictionaries en_US, en_GB-ise and en_CA from
  https://wordlist.aspell.net, bundled unmodified. Their upstream notice (embedded too, for the
  About window) is language-data/en-us/LICENCE-SCOWL.txt in craft-i18n. The word lists:

    Copyright 2000-2026 by Kevin Atkinson

    Permission to use, copy, modify, distribute, and sell any part of SCOWLv2, or
    word lists created from it, is hereby granted without fee, provided that the
    above copyright notice appears in all copies and that both the above
    copyright notice and this notice appear in supporting documentation.  Kevin
    Atkinson makes no representations about the suitability of this database for
    any purpose.  It is provided "as is" without express or implied warranty.

  The Australian data:

    Copyright 2016 by Benjamin Titze

    Permission to use, copy, modify, distribute and sell this array, the
    associated software, and its documentation for any purpose is hereby
    granted without fee, provided that the above copyright notice appears
    in all copies and that both that copyright notice and this permission
    notice appear in supporting documentation. Benjamin Titze makes no
    representations about the suitability of this array for any
    purpose. It is provided "as is" without express or implied warranty.

    Copyright 1993, Geoff Kuenning, Granada Hills, CA
    All rights reserved.

- Application-drawn UI icons (crates/ui-egui/src/icons.rs) are original work, MIT OR Apache-2.0;
  the bundled egui font icons have the separate notice below.

Rust dependencies (egui, eframe, vello_cpu, skrifa, harfrust, krilla, quick-xml, zip, image, …)
are permissively licensed (MIT/Apache-2.0/BSD/ISC/Zlib) and fetched through crates.io or pinned Git sources.
The craft-pdf writer and Krilla 0.8.2 backend are pinned to storytold/craft-libs revision
b926b5c03e256c86ca10934f58d03f628810b5b7. The backend retains its MIT OR Apache-2.0 licensing
and upstream bundled material; see that revision's licenses/pdf-writer/ and ATTRIBUTION.md.
The backend patch prevents finalization during an unwind and rejects a writer after failed
cleanup. WordCraft enables only writing; PDF import and its image decoders are not enabled.

No Microsoft, Adobe, Avid or Autodesk icons, images, templates or artwork are included.

Bundled egui font icons

The canonical egui0.37 dependency includes egui-icons.ttf (a small subset of emoji-icon-font)
through default_fonts, and emoji-icon-font.ttf through monochrome_emoji_fonts. Both are embedded
by the UI font definitions. Their upstream project is https://github.com/jslegers/emoji-icon-font;
the exact copyright and permission notice from the pinned egui dependency follows.

----------------------------------------
epaint_default_fonts 0.37 embeds egui-icons.ttf, a five-glyph subset of
emoji-icon-font by John Slegers. The full monochrome emoji-icon-font fallback
uses the same MIT license. Upstream license text follows unchanged.

----------------------------------

https://github.com/storytold/egui/tree/6d518fc799681571963842f95b55a6b68431b15a/crates/epaint_default_fonts/fonts,

`monochrome_emoji_fonts` feature is enabled. The font's copyright notice and the

Linux Wayland window decorations
-------------------------------
sctk-adwaita 0.10.1 (https://github.com/PolyMeilex/sctk-adwaita,
commit d5f343c45c04d4a026db57f76760819b7325db00) is used by winit on Linux
for vector window controls. Its title-font features are disabled: no Cantarell
font is embedded by this dependency and no font file is added to this repo.

Code dependencies

- symphonia (audio decoding for MP3, AAC, ALAC, FLAC, Vorbis, WAV, AIFF, CAF) is used unmodified
  under the Mozilla Public License 2.0; its source is available at
  https://github.com/pdeljanov/Symphonia.
- The H.264, HEVC, VP9 and AV1 crates under crates/ are ported from FilmCraft (same owner,
  MIT OR Apache-2.0). The Opus decoder (craft-audio) and the MP4, Matroska and Ogg demuxers
  (craft-media) come from craft-libs (same owner, MIT OR Apache-2.0).

- Krilla 0.8.2 (https://github.com/LaurenzV/krilla) is used through craft-pdf under
  MIT OR Apache-2.0. craft-libs vendors that version with page/surface unwind recovery
  and rejection of serialization after failed cleanup. Its upstream licence files are
  retained in craft-libs/licenses/pdf-writer. Its bundled Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram
  (https://github.com/saucecontrol/Compact-ICC-Profiles) are CC0-1.0; the profile
  licence is retained in licenses/pdf-writer/LICENSE-ICC.txt and exact files, sources
  and hashes are in ATTRIBUTION.md.


egui icon font subset (dependency asset)
----------------------------------------
epaint_default_fonts 0.37 embeds egui-icons.ttf, a five-glyph subset of
emoji-icon-font by John Slegers. The full monochrome emoji-icon-font fallback
uses the same MIT license. Upstream license text follows unchanged.

The regional format data in crates/core/src/region.rs (separators, month and weekday names, AM/PM
words, currency symbols and date order) follows the Unicode Common Locale Data Repository (CLDR),
read from unicode-org/cldr-json and from ICU's CLDR 48 data, under the Unicode License v3.
Source: https://github.com/unicode-org/cldr-json

Linux Wayland window decorations
-------------------------------
sctk-adwaita 0.10.1 (https://github.com/PolyMeilex/sctk-adwaita,
commit d5f343c45c04d4a026db57f76760819b7325db00) is used by winit on Linux
for vector window controls. Its title-font features are disabled: no Cantarell
font is embedded by this dependency and no font file is added to this repo.

Third-party material

This product bundles third-party material under its own licenses, listed below. The per-file

- Archivo (assets/fonts/) — SIL Open Font License 1.1. See assets/fonts/OFL-Archivo.txt.
- Geist Mono (assets/fonts/) — SIL Open Font License 1.1. See assets/fonts/OFL-GeistMono.txt.

  - Ubuntu Light — Ubuntu Font Licence 1.0.
  - Noto Emoji — SIL Open Font License 1.1.
  - Hack — MIT License; contains Bitstream Vera glyphs under the Bitstream Vera License.
  - emoji-icon-font (Copyright (c) 2014 John Slegers) — MIT License.
  The licence texts ship in that crate's fonts/ folder (UFL.txt, OFL.txt, Hack-Regular.txt,
  emoji-icon-font-mit-license.txt) and in the third-party licence bundle (docs/licences.md).

Rust crates are listed with their licences in the third-party licence bundle shipped with each
release (see docs/licences.md).

Builds made with craft-fonts (CRAFT_FONTS_DIR) also embed the fonts listed in ATTRIBUTION.md.
